Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The gravimetry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 119. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 577 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 119 + O(1/n^577). Summing over all modes and applying the gravimetry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
